## C04. Does bare “by” establish receipt rather than postmark?

`wa-policy-70d5b3ac192882a9f5c494bb3dbe35d3c8280f1e01608fc93ec87183bd93f22e`; 24 instances.

Proposed: Preserve explicit received/postmarked conditions only; bare “by” stays literal with null receipt condition.

A temporal endpoint alone does not select a delivery condition; every affected original remains enumerated.

`wa-policy-3eba53c10f498c73e5f5ddb271f88420417a6b8743086f6db6c90b958b6fde6c`; 49 instances.

Proposed: Allow new_notice plus carry_in where the stated window opened before June 3 and remained active then. Preserve the printed opening and issue date.

The common brief expressly permits overlapping cohorts. Filing/opening and authoritative publication are distinct.

## C06. Use the current filing when no earlier root is explicitly cited?

`wa-policy-ca59e1c6e47c00051a2401b15aa5cd74b2049ca36ac1a7571401a7381d65dd00`; 61 instances.

Proposed: Keep current WSR key provisionally; do not merge by title. Same evidence-based identity principle as VA C02 (pending owner signature).

The frozen WA tuple requires the earliest explicitly cited initiating chain root; historical amendatory citations are not initiating roots.
